# Collection catalogue — Open Christian Data v1.0.0

**852 works · 881 selected editions · 711,061 records**

Every work and copy included in this release is listed below. A work can be a collection; its individual sermons, hymns, or letters are not necessarily separate catalog entries.

[Download the edition list (CSV)](WORK_CATALOG.csv) · [MODS bibliography (XML)](WORK_CATALOG.mods.xml) · [Catalog fields and limits](CATALOG_GUIDE.md)

Browse by title within each download group. Authors and other contributors are listed where known, with publication details and links to the digital sources. The CSV and XML downloads preserve the full recorded descriptions and identifiers.

## Download groups

| Group | Works | Editions | Records |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
| [bible](#bible) | 21 | 21 | 619,210 |
| [catechisms](#catechisms) | 14 | 16 | 1,448 |
| [commentary](#commentary) | 40 | 61 | 10,981 |
| [devotionals](#devotionals) | 1 | 1 | 732 |
| [doctrinal_documents](#doctrinal-documents) | 27 | 27 | 1,236 |
| [hymns](#hymns) | 7 | 7 | 37,920 |
| [liturgies](#liturgies) | 5 | 5 | 78 |
| [poetry](#poetry) | 62 | 62 | 208 |
| [prayers](#prayers) | 7 | 7 | 135 |
| [prose](#prose) | 650 | 655 | 5,093 |
| [reference_entries](#reference-entries) | 4 | 4 | 22,774 |
| [sermons](#sermons) | 12 | 13 | 5,301 |
| [topical_indexes](#topical-indexes) | 2 | 2 | 5,945 |
